## 2026-10-16T06:4xZ — Outbound self-trigger found and closed at source
- Two consecutive Hetz-route watcher signals this morning were **our own outbound mirrored back as inbound**, not Hetz replying. Proof: the woken sha `48c0010e5d08` is the controller-answers file authored here, and `ls` shows **no** Hetz file newer than our 05:38:53Z send. Treating these as "Hetz replied" would have produced a fabricated answer.
- **Loop, traced in `/root/bin/hetz-comms-sync.sh`:** push outbound into Hetz's `controller-replies/` (line 40) -> pull that same folder back (line 27) -> `scan` it as inbound (line 32). So every outbound message self-wakes, **twice**, because one message carries two filenames and dedup is sha **plus** name. The mirror contained weeks of our own messages already logged as `comms-received` from Hetz.
- Fix is an exclusion boundary in the ingest, not a suppression: `FM_COMMS_EXCLUDE_NAMES_FROM` causes `fm-comms-ingest.sh` to skip scanned basenames this home authored and to **print** the skip. Wired only into the `controller-replies/` scan; `from-brandon-compute/` is Hetz's real output and is untouched. RED proven first, then GREEN. Ingest suite green, comms-notify still 16/16.
- Test-hygiene defect fixed en route: I asserted with `assert_absent`, which checks that a **path** does not exist, where a content check was required; `assert_no_grep` is the content helper. The case was also made hermetic after a prior failing run left a status file in the shared tmp root.
- Live proof: the wired exclusion over the real mirror excluded every one of our own outbound files and the wake queue gained **0** lines.
- Queue-noise item is now closed at the source. Still open and **not** claimed: Gemini on HD2 `wK:p2` is dead with no report and no native Pi To-Do in that runtime; Pi 1.3.0 extension compatibility and the live stop/handoff trip remain unproven.

Call site hardened from `if rc==1 / elif rc==2` to a `case` that **holds on ANY non-0 rc**. RED `PASS=22 FAIL=4` → pre-fix reconstruction `PASS=19 FAIL=8` (fail-open signature observed: rc=127 where rc=1 was required) → GREEN `PASS=27 FAIL=0`; `bash -n` clean; temp fixtures removed.
